# Intrinsics in Alphabetical Order

1665 intrinsics, one page each. See also the grouped pages linked from the home page.

LSX and LASX intrinsics are grouped by the first letter of the operation name (the letter after the `v`/`xv` prefix), for example `__lsx_vadd_b` is listed under LSX_A.

## LSX_A (64)

- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_b/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_bu/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_d/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_du/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_h/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_hu/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_w/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vabsd_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vabsd_wu/index.md) — Compute the absolute difference between corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadd_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadd_b/index.md) — Add corresponding 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadd_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadd_d/index.md) — Add corresponding 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadd_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadd_h/index.md) — Add corresponding 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadd_q](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadd_q/index.md) — Add corresponding 128-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadd_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadd_w/index.md) — Add corresponding 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadda_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadda_b/index.md) — Add the absolute values of the 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadda_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadda_d/index.md) — Add the absolute values of the 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadda_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadda_h/index.md) — Add the absolute values of the 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vadda_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vadda_w/index.md) — Add the absolute values of the 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddi_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddi_bu/index.md) — Add the zero-extended `imm` to each 8-bit element in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddi_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddi_du/index.md) — Add the zero-extended `imm` to each 64-bit element in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddi_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddi_hu/index.md) — Add the zero-extended `imm` to each 16-bit element in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddi_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddi_wu/index.md) — Add the zero-extended `imm` to each 32-bit element in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_d_w/index.md) — Add the even-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_d_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_d_wu/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_d_wu_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_d_wu_w/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_h_b/index.md) — Add the even-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_h_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_h_bu/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_h_bu_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_h_bu_b/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_q_d/index.md) — Add the even-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_q_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_q_du/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_q_du_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_q_du_d/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_w_h/index.md) — Add the even-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_w_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_w_hu/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwev_w_hu_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwev_w_hu_h/index.md) — Add the even-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_d_w/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_d_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_d_wu/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_d_wu_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_d_wu_w/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_h_b/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_h_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_h_bu/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_h_bu_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_h_bu_b/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_q_d/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_q_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_q_du/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_q_du_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_q_du_d/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_w_h/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_w_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_w_hu/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vaddwod_w_hu_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vaddwod_w_hu_h/index.md) — Add the odd-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vand_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vand_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise AND of elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vandi_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vandi_b/index.md) — Compute bitwise AND of elements in `a` and `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vandn_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vandn_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise ANDN (~a & b) of elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_b/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_bu/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_d/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_du/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_h/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_hu/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_w/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavg_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavg_wu/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards negative infinity) of corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_b/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_bu/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_d/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_du/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_h/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_hu/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_w/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vavgr_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vavgr_wu/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.

## LSX_B (38)

- [\_\_lsx_bnz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bnz_b/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 8-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bnz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bnz_d/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 64-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bnz_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bnz_h/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 16-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bnz_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bnz_v/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if the whole vector `a` is non-zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bnz_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bnz_w/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 32-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bz_b/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 8-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bz_d/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 64-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bz_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bz_h/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 16-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bz_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bz_v/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if the whole vector `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lsx_bz_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_bz_w/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 32-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclr_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclr_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 64, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclr_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 16, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclr_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 32, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclri_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclri_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclri_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclri_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclri_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclri_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitclri_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitclri_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrev_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrev_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrev_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrev_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 64, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrev_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrev_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 16, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrev_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrev_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 32, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrevi_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrevi_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrevi_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrevi_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrevi_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrevi_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitrevi_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitrevi_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitsel_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitsel_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise selection: for each bit position, if the bit in `c` equals to one, copy the bit from `b` to `dst`, otherwise copy from `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitseli_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitseli_b/index.md) — Use each bit of `a` as a mask: where the bit is 1, select the corresponding bit of `imm`; where it is 0, select the corresponding bit of `b`. The result is written to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitset_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitset_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitset_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitset_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 64, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitset_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitset_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 16, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitset_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitset_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 32, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitseti_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitseti_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitseti_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitseti_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitseti_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitseti_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbitseti_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbitseti_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vbsll_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbsll_v/index.md) — Shift each 128-bit lane of `a` left by `(imm % 16) * 8` bits, filling the vacated bits with zeros.
- [\_\_lsx_vbsrl_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vbsrl_v/index.md) — Shift each 128-bit lane of `a` right by `(imm % 16) * 8` bits, filling the vacated bits with zeros.

## LSX_C (8)

- [\_\_lsx_vclo_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclo_b/index.md) — Count leading ones of 8-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vclo_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclo_d/index.md) — Count leading ones of 64-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vclo_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclo_h/index.md) — Count leading ones of 16-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vclo_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclo_w/index.md) — Count leading ones of 32-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vclz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclz_b/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 8-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vclz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclz_d/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 64-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vclz_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclz_h/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 16-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vclz_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vclz_w/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 32-bit elements in `a`.

## LSX_D (8)

- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_b/index.md) — Divide each signed 8-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_bu/index.md) — Divide each unsigned 8-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_d/index.md) — Divide each signed 64-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_du/index.md) — Divide each unsigned 64-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_h/index.md) — Divide each signed 16-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_hu/index.md) — Divide each unsigned 16-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_w/index.md) — Divide each signed 32-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vdiv_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vdiv_wu/index.md) — Divide each unsigned 32-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.

## LSX_E (14)

- [\_\_lsx_vexth_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_d_w/index.md) — Sign-extend 32-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 64-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vexth_du_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_du_wu/index.md) — Zero-extend 32-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 64-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vexth_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_h_b/index.md) — Sign-extend 8-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 16-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vexth_hu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_hu_bu/index.md) — Zero-extend 8-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 16-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vexth_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_q_d/index.md) — Sign-extend 64-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vexth_qu_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_qu_du/index.md) — Zero-extend 64-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vexth_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_w_h/index.md) — Sign-extend 16-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 32-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vexth_wu_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vexth_wu_hu/index.md) — Zero-extend 16-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 32-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vextl_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vextl_q_d/index.md) — Sign-extend 64-bit elements in the lower half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vextl_qu_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vextl_qu_du/index.md) — Zero-extend 64-bit elements in the lower half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lsx_vextrins_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vextrins_b/index.md) — Copy the 8-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[3:0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[7:4]`; other elements remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vextrins_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vextrins_d/index.md) — Copy the 64-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[4]`; other elements remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vextrins_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vextrins_h/index.md) — Copy the 16-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[2:0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[6:4]`; other elements remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vextrins_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vextrins_w/index.md) — Copy the 32-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[1:0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[5:4]`; other elements remain unchanged.

- [\_\_lsx_vfcvt_h_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfcvt_h_s/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert single-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as half-precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfcvt_s_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfcvt_s_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as single-precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfcvth_d_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfcvth_d_s/index.md) — Convert single precision floating point elements in higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to double precision.
- [\_\_lsx_vfcvth_s_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfcvth_s_h/index.md) — Convert half precision floating point elements in higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to single precision.
- [\_\_lsx_vfcvtl_d_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfcvtl_d_s/index.md) — Convert single precision floating point elements in lower half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to double precision.
- [\_\_lsx_vfcvtl_s_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfcvtl_s_h/index.md) — Convert half precision floating point elements in lower half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to single precision.
- [\_\_lsx_vfdiv_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfdiv_d/index.md) — Divide corresponding double precision floating point elements in `a` by elements in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfdiv_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfdiv_s/index.md) — Divide corresponding single precision floating point elements in `a` by elements in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vffint_d_l](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vffint_d_l/index.md) — Convert signed 64-bit integer elements in `a` to double-precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vffint_d_lu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vffint_d_lu/index.md) — Convert unsigned 64-bit integer elements in `a` to double-precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vffint_s_l](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vffint_s_l/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert signed 64-bit integer elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as single-precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vffint_s_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vffint_s_w/index.md) — Convert signed 32-bit integer elements in `a` to single-precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vffint_s_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vffint_s_wu/index.md) — Convert unsigned 32-bit integer elements in `a` to single-precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vffinth_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vffinth_d_w/index.md) — Convert signed 32-bit integer elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to double precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vffintl_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vffintl_d_w/index.md) — Convert signed 32-bit integer elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to double precision floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vflogb_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vflogb_d/index.md) — Compute base-2 logarithm of double precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vflogb_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vflogb_s/index.md) — Compute base-2 logarithm of single precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmadd_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmadd_d/index.md) — Compute packed double precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, accumulate to elements in `c` and store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmadd_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmadd_s/index.md) — Compute packed single precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, accumulate to elements in `c` and store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmax_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmax_d/index.md) — Compute the maximum (maxNum) of double precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmax_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmax_s/index.md) — Compute the maximum (maxNum) of single precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmaxa_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmaxa_d/index.md) — Compute the maximum (maxNumMag) of double precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`, selecting the element with the larger absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmaxa_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmaxa_s/index.md) — Compute the maximum (maxNumMag) of single precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`, selecting the element with the larger absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmin_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmin_d/index.md) — Compute the minimum (minNum) of double precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmin_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmin_s/index.md) — Compute the minimum (minNum) of single precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmina_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmina_d/index.md) — Compute the minimum (minNumMag) of double precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`, selecting the element with the smaller absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmina_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmina_s/index.md) — Compute the minimum (minNumMag) of single precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`, selecting the element with the smaller absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmsub_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmsub_d/index.md) — Compute packed double precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, subtract elements in `c` and store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmsub_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmsub_s/index.md) — Compute packed single precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, subtract elements in `c` and store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmul_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmul_d/index.md) — Multiply corresponding double precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfmul_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfmul_s/index.md) — Multiply corresponding single precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfnmadd_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfnmadd_d/index.md) — Compute packed double precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, accumulate to elements in `c` and store the negated result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfnmadd_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfnmadd_s/index.md) — Compute packed single precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, accumulate to elements in `c` and store the negated result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfnmsub_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfnmsub_d/index.md) — Compute packed double precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, subtract elements in `c` and store the negated result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfnmsub_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfnmsub_s/index.md) — Compute packed single precision floating point FMA(Fused Multiply-Add): multiply elements in `a` and `b`, subtract elements in `c` and store the negated result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrecip_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrecip_d/index.md) — Compute reciprocal of double precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrecip_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrecip_s/index.md) — Compute reciprocal of single precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrecipe_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrecipe_d/index.md) — Compute estimated reciprocal of double precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrecipe_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrecipe_s/index.md) — Compute estimated reciprocal of single precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrint_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrint_d/index.md) — Round double-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrint_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrint_s/index.md) — Round single-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrm_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrm_d/index.md) — Round double-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards negative infinity, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrm_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrm_s/index.md) — Round single-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards negative infinity, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrne_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrne_d/index.md) — Round double-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards nearest even, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrne_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrne_s/index.md) — Round single-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards nearest even, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrp_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrp_d/index.md) — Round double-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards positive infinity, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrp_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrp_s/index.md) — Round single-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards positive infinity, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrz_d/index.md) — Round double-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards zero, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrintrz_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrintrz_s/index.md) — Round single-precision floating point elements in `a` to integers, rounding towards zero, and store as floating point numbers.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrsqrt_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrsqrt_d/index.md) — Compute reciprocal of square root of double precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrsqrt_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrsqrt_s/index.md) — Compute reciprocal of square root of single precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrsqrte_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrsqrte_d/index.md) — Compute estimated reciprocal of square root of double precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrsqrte_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrsqrte_s/index.md) — Compute estimated reciprocal of square root of single precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrstp_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrstp_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 8-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by the first element of the corresponding lane of `c` modulo 16, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrstp_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrstp_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 16-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by the first element of the corresponding lane of `c` modulo 8, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrstpi_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrstpi_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 8-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by `imm` modulo 16, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vfrstpi_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfrstpi_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 16-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by `imm` modulo 8, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vfscaleb_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfscaleb_d/index.md) — Compute IEEE 754 scaleB (multiply by 2^`b`) of double precision floating point elements in `a` using integer exponents in `b`. Currently undocumented.
- [\_\_lsx_vfscaleb_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfscaleb_s/index.md) — Compute IEEE 754 scaleB (multiply by 2^`b`) of single precision floating point elements in `a` using integer exponents in `b`. Currently undocumented.
- [\_\_lsx_vfsqrt_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfsqrt_d/index.md) — Compute square root of double precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfsqrt_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfsqrt_s/index.md) — Compute square root of single precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfsub_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfsub_d/index.md) — Subtract corresponding double precision floating point elements in `b` from elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vfsub_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vfsub_s/index.md) — Subtract corresponding single precision floating point elements in `b` from elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftint_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftint_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftint_lu_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftint_lu_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to unsigned 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftint_w_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftint_w_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as signed 32-bit integers, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftint_w_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftint_w_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 32-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftint_wu_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftint_wu_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to unsigned 32-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftinth_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftinth_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintl_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintl_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrm_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrm_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrm_w_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrm_w_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as signed 32-bit integers, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrm_w_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrm_w_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 32-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrmh_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrmh_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrml_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrml_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrne_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrne_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards nearest even.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrne_w_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrne_w_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as signed 32-bit integers, rounding towards nearest even.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrne_w_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrne_w_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 32-bit integer, rounding towards nearest even.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrneh_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrneh_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards nearest even.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrnel_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrnel_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards nearest even.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrp_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrp_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards positive infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrp_w_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrp_w_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as signed 32-bit integers, rounding towards positive infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrp_w_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrp_w_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 32-bit integer, rounding towards positive infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrph_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrph_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards positive infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrpl_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrpl_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards positive infinity.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrz_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrz_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrz_lu_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrz_lu_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to unsigned 64-bit integer, rounding towards zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrz_w_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrz_w_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as signed 32-bit integers, rounding towards zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrz_w_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrz_w_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 32-bit integer, rounding towards zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrz_wu_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrz_wu_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to unsigned 32-bit integer, rounding towards zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrzh_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrzh_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vftintrzl_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vftintrzl_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards zero.

## LSX_H (16)

- [\_\_lsx_vhaddw_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhaddw_d_w/index.md) — Add odd-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `b`, sign-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lsx_vhaddw_du_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhaddw_du_wu/index.md) — Add odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `b`, zero-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lsx_vhaddw_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhaddw_h_b/index.md) — Add odd-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `b`, sign-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.
- [\_\_lsx_vhaddw_hu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhaddw_hu_bu/index.md) — Add odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `b`, zero-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.

- [\_\_lsx_vhaddw_wu_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhaddw_wu_hu/index.md) — Add odd-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `b`, zero-extending both to 32-bit, to get the 32-bit result.
- [\_\_lsx_vhsubw_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhsubw_d_w/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a`, sign-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lsx_vhsubw_du_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhsubw_du_wu/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a`, zero-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lsx_vhsubw_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhsubw_h_b/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a`, sign-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.
- [\_\_lsx_vhsubw_hu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vhsubw_hu_bu/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a`, zero-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.

## LSX_I (12)

- [\_\_lsx_vilvh_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvh_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 8-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vilvh_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvh_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 64-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vilvh_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvh_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 16-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vilvh_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvh_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 32-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vilvl_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvl_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 8-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vilvl_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvl_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 64-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vilvl_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvl_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 16-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vilvl_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vilvl_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 32-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vinsgr2vr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vinsgr2vr_b/index.md) — Insert the low 8 bits of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vinsgr2vr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vinsgr2vr_d/index.md) — Insert the low 64 bits of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vinsgr2vr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vinsgr2vr_h/index.md) — Insert the low 16 bits of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.
- [\_\_lsx_vinsgr2vr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vinsgr2vr_w/index.md) — Insert the low 32 bits of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.

## LSX_L (7)

- [\_\_lsx_vld](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vld/index.md) — Read whole vector from memory address `addr + offset`, save the data into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. Note that you can use this intrinsic to load floating point vectors, even though the return type represents integer vectors.
- [\_\_lsx_vldi](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vldi/index.md) — Initialize `dst` using predefined patterns:
- [\_\_lsx_vldrepl_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vldrepl_b/index.md) — Read 8-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lsx_vldrepl_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vldrepl_d/index.md) — Read 64-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lsx_vldrepl_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vldrepl_h/index.md) — Read 16-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lsx_vldrepl_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vldrepl_w/index.md) — Read 32-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lsx_vldx](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vldx/index.md) — Read whole vector from memory address `addr + offset`, save the data into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. Note that you can use this intrinsic to load floating point vectors, even though the return type represents integer vectors.

## LSX_M (115)

- [\_\_lsx_vmadd_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmadd_b/index.md) — Multiply 8-bit elements in `b` and `c`, add the low 8 bits of the product to elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmadd_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmadd_d/index.md) — Multiply 64-bit elements in `b` and `c`, add the low 64 bits of the product to elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmadd_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmadd_h/index.md) — Multiply 16-bit elements in `b` and `c`, add the low 16 bits of the product to elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmadd_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmadd_w/index.md) — Multiply 32-bit elements in `b` and `c`, add the low 32 bits of the product to elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmaddwev_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmaddwev_d_w/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `b` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `c`, extending both to 64-bit, and add the product to the 64-bit elements in `a`.

- [\_\_lsx_vmaddwev_d_wu_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmaddwev_d_wu_w/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `b` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `c`, extending both to 64-bit, and add the product to the 64-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmaddwev_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmaddwev_h_b/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `b` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `c`, extending both to 16-bit, and add the product to the 16-bit elements in `a`.

- [\_\_lsx_vmaddwev_h_bu_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmaddwev_h_bu_b/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `b` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `c`, extending both to 16-bit, and add the product to the 16-bit elements in `a`.

- [\_\_lsx_vmax_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmax_du/index.md) — Compute elementwise maximum for corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmax_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmax_h/index.md) — Compute elementwise maximum for corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmax_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmax_hu/index.md) — Compute elementwise maximum for corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmax_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmax_w/index.md) — Compute elementwise maximum for corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`.

- [\_\_lsx_vmin_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmin_b/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`.

- [\_\_lsx_vmin_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmin_h/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmin_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmin_hu/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmin_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmin_w/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmin_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmin_wu/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_b/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for signed 8-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_bu/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the zero-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_d/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for signed 64-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_du/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the zero-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_h/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for signed 16-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_hu/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and the zero-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_w/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for signed 32-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmini_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmini_wu/index.md) — Compute elementwise minimum for unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the zero-extended `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_b/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each signed 8-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_bu/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each unsigned 8-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_d/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each signed 64-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_du/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each unsigned 64-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_h/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each signed 16-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_hu/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each unsigned 16-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_w/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each signed 32-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmod_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmod_wu/index.md) — Compute the remainder of each unsigned 32-bit element in `a` divided by the corresponding element in `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmskgez_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmskgez_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, for each 8-bit element in `a`, set the bit at that element's index within the lane to one if the element is greater than or equal to zero, otherwise zero; the remaining bits of the lane are zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vmskltz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmskltz_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, for each 8-bit element in `a`, set the bit at that element's index within the lane to one if the element is less than zero, otherwise zero; the remaining bits of the lane are zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vmskltz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmskltz_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, for each 64-bit element in `a`, set the bit at that element's index within the lane to one if the element is less than zero, otherwise zero; the remaining bits of the lane are zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vmskltz_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmskltz_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, for each 16-bit element in `a`, set the bit at that element's index within the lane to one if the element is less than zero, otherwise zero; the remaining bits of the lane are zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vmskltz_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmskltz_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, for each 32-bit element in `a`, set the bit at that element's index within the lane to one if the element is less than zero, otherwise zero; the remaining bits of the lane are zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vmsknz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmsknz_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, for each 8-bit element in `a`, set the bit at that element's index within the lane to one if the element is non-zero, otherwise zero; the remaining bits of the lane are zero.
- [\_\_lsx_vmsub_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmsub_b/index.md) — Multiply 8-bit elements in `b` and `c`, subtract the low 8 bits of the product from elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmsub_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmsub_d/index.md) — Multiply 64-bit elements in `b` and `c`, subtract the low 64 bits of the product from elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmsub_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmsub_h/index.md) — Multiply 16-bit elements in `b` and `c`, subtract the low 16 bits of the product from elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmsub_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmsub_w/index.md) — Multiply 32-bit elements in `b` and `c`, subtract the low 32 bits of the product from elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmuh_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmuh_b/index.md) — Multiply corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the upper half of the double-width product in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmuh_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmuh_bu/index.md) — Multiply corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the upper half of the double-width product in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmuh_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmuh_d/index.md) — Multiply corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the upper half of the double-width product in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmuh_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmuh_du/index.md) — Multiply corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the upper half of the double-width product in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmuh_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmuh_h/index.md) — Multiply corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the upper half of the double-width product in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmuh_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmuh_hu/index.md) — Multiply corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the upper half of the double-width product in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmuh_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmuh_w/index.md) — Multiply corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the upper half of the double-width product in `dst`.
