- [\_\_lsx_vmul_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmul_b/index.md) — Multiply corresponding 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmul_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmul_d/index.md) — Multiply corresponding 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmul_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmul_h/index.md) — Multiply corresponding 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmul_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmul_w/index.md) — Multiply corresponding 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_d_w/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_d_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_d_wu/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_d_wu_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_d_wu_w/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_h_b/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_h_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_h_bu/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_h_bu_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_h_bu_b/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_q_d/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_q_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_q_du/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_q_du_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_q_du_d/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_w_h/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_w_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_w_hu/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwev_w_hu_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwev_w_hu_h/index.md) — Multiply the even-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_d_w/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_d_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_d_wu/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_d_wu_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_d_wu_w/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 64-bit, and save the 64-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_h_b/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_h_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_h_bu/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_h_bu_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_h_bu_b/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 8-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 16-bit, and save the 16-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_q_d/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_q_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_q_du/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vmulwod_q_du_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vmulwod_q_du_d/index.md) — Multiply the odd-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and the corresponding signed 64-bit elements in `b`, extending both to 128-bit, and save the 128-bit result in `dst`.

## LSX_N (6)

- [\_\_lsx_vneg_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vneg_b/index.md) — Negate 8-bit elements in `a` and save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vneg_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vneg_d/index.md) — Negate 64-bit elements in `a` and save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vneg_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vneg_h/index.md) — Negate 16-bit elements in `a` and save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vneg_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vneg_w/index.md) — Negate 32-bit elements in `a` and save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vnor_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vnor_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise NOR (~(a | b)) of elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vnori_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vnori_b/index.md) — Compute bitwise NOR (~(a | imm)) of elements in `a` and `imm`.

## LSX_O (3)

- [\_\_lsx_vor_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vor_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise OR of elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lsx_vori_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vori_b/index.md) — Compute bitwise OR of elements in `a` and `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vorn_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vorn_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise ORN (a | ~b) of elements in `a` and `b`.

## LSX_P (29)

- [\_\_lsx_vpackev_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackev_b/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 8-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpackev_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackev_d/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpackev_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackev_h/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpackev_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackev_w/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 32-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpackod_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackod_b/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 8-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpackod_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackod_d/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpackod_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackod_h/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpackod_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpackod_w/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 32-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lsx_vpcnt_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpcnt_b/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 8-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpcnt_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpcnt_d/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 64-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpcnt_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpcnt_h/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 16-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpcnt_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpcnt_w/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 32-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpermi_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpermi_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, use `imm[1:0]` and `imm[3:2]` to select words 0 and 1 from `b`, and `imm[5:4]` and `imm[7:6]` to select words 2 and 3 from `a`; store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickev_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickev_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place even-positioned 8-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and even-positioned 8-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickev_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickev_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place even-positioned 64-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and even-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickev_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickev_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place even-positioned 16-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and even-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.

- [\_\_lsx_vpickod_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickod_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place odd-positioned 64-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and odd-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickod_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickod_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place odd-positioned 16-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and odd-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickod_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickod_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place odd-positioned 32-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and odd-positioned 32-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_b/index.md) — Pick the 8-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, sign-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_bu/index.md) — Pick the 8-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, zero-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_d/index.md) — Pick the 64-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, sign-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_du/index.md) — Pick the 64-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, zero-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_h/index.md) — Pick the 16-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, sign-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_hu/index.md) — Pick the 16-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, zero-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_w/index.md) — Pick the 32-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, sign-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vpickve2gr_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vpickve2gr_wu/index.md) — Pick the 32-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, zero-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.

## LSX_R (24)

- [\_\_lsx_vreplgr2vr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplgr2vr_b/index.md) — Broadcast the low 8 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplgr2vr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplgr2vr_d/index.md) — Broadcast the low 64 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplgr2vr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplgr2vr_h/index.md) — Broadcast the low 16 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplgr2vr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplgr2vr_w/index.md) — Broadcast the low 32 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrepli_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrepli_b/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vrepli_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrepli_d/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vrepli_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrepli_h/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vrepli_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrepli_w/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplve_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplve_b/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` (index modulo 16) of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplve_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplve_d/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` (index modulo 2) of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplve_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplve_h/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` (index modulo 8) of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplve_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplve_w/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` (index modulo 4) of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplvei_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplvei_b/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplvei_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplvei_d/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplvei_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplvei_h/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vreplvei_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vreplvei_w/index.md) — Repeat the element in lane `idx` of `a` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotr_b/index.md) — Rotate right each unsigned 8-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotr_d/index.md) — Rotate right each unsigned 64-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 64, store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotr_h/index.md) — Rotate right each unsigned 16-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 16, store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotr_w/index.md) — Rotate right each unsigned 32-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 32, store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotri_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotri_b/index.md) — Rotate right the unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` by `imm`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotri_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotri_d/index.md) — Rotate right the unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` by `imm`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotri_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotri_h/index.md) — Rotate right the unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` by `imm`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vrotri_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vrotri_w/index.md) — Rotate right the unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` by `imm`, store the result to `dst`.

- [\_\_lsx_vsadd_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsadd_du/index.md) — Saturating add corresponding unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsadd_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsadd_h/index.md) — Saturating add corresponding signed 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsadd_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsadd_hu/index.md) — Saturating add corresponding unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsadd_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsadd_w/index.md) — Saturating add corresponding signed 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsadd_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsadd_wu/index.md) — Saturating add corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_b/index.md) — Saturate each signed 8-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_bu/index.md) — Saturate each unsigned 8-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_d/index.md) — Saturate each signed 64-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_du/index.md) — Saturate each unsigned 64-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_h/index.md) — Saturate each signed 16-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_hu/index.md) — Saturate each unsigned 16-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_w/index.md) — Saturate each signed 32-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsat_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsat_wu/index.md) — Saturate each unsigned 32-bit element in `a` to the number of significant bits specified by `imm`.
- [\_\_lsx_vseq_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseq_b/index.md) — Compare the 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vseq_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseq_d/index.md) — Compare the 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vseq_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseq_h/index.md) — Compare the 16-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vseq_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseq_w/index.md) — Compare the 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vseqi_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseqi_b/index.md) — Compare the 8-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vseqi_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseqi_d/index.md) — Compare the 64-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vseqi_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseqi_h/index.md) — Compare the 16-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vseqi_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vseqi_w/index.md) — Compare the 32-bit elements in `a` and the sign-extended `imm`, store all-ones to `dst` if equal, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf4i_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf4i_b/index.md) — For each group of four consecutive 8-bit elements in `a`, permute the group according to the four 2-bit fields of `imm`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf4i_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf4i_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, select two 64-bit elements from the four elements formed by the corresponding lanes of `a` and `b` using `imm[1:0]` and `imm[3:2]`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf4i_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf4i_h/index.md) — For each group of four consecutive 16-bit elements in `a`, permute the group according to the four 2-bit fields of `imm`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf4i_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf4i_w/index.md) — For each group of four consecutive 32-bit elements in `a`, permute the group according to the four 2-bit fields of `imm`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle bytes from `a` and `b` using the low 5 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `c`.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle 64-bit elements from `b` and `c` using the low 2 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `a`, and save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle 16-bit elements from `b` and `c` using the low 4 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `a`, and save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vshuf_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vshuf_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle 32-bit elements from `b` and `c` using the low 3 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `a`, and save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lsx_vsigncov_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsigncov_b/index.md) — If the 8-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 8-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vsigncov_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsigncov_d/index.md) — If the 64-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 64-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vsigncov_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsigncov_h/index.md) — If the 16-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 16-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vsigncov_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsigncov_w/index.md) — If the 32-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 32-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.
- [\_\_lsx_vsle_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsle_b/index.md) — Compare the signed 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if corresponding element in `a` is less than or equal to `b`, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vsle_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsle_bu/index.md) — Compare the unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if corresponding element in `a` is less than or equal to `b`, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vsle_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsle_d/index.md) — Compare the signed 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if corresponding element in `a` is less than or equal to `b`, zero otherwise.
- [\_\_lsx_vsle_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsle_du/index.md) — Compare the unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if corresponding element in `a` is less than or equal to `b`, zero otherwise.

- [\_\_lsx_vsle_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vsle_wu/index.md) — Compare the unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, store all-ones to `dst` if corresponding element in `a` is less than or equal to `b`, zero otherwise.

- [\_\_lsx_vst](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vst/index.md) — Write whole vector data in `data` to memory address `addr + offset`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception.
- [\_\_lsx_vstelm_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vstelm_b/index.md) — Store element `lane` of `data` to memory address `addr + offset`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lsx_vstelm_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vstelm_d/index.md) — Store element `lane` of `data` to memory address `addr + offset`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.

- [\_\_lsx_vstelm_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vstelm_w/index.md) — Store element `lane` of `data` to memory address `addr + offset`. - [\_\_lsx_vstx](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lsx_vstx/index.md) — Write whole vector data in `data` to memory address `addr + offset`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception.

- [\_\_lasx_xvavgr_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvavgr_wu/index.md) — Compute the average (rounded towards positive infinity) of corresponding unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` and `b`, save the result in `dst`.

## LASX_B (38)

- [\_\_lasx_xbnz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbnz_b/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 8-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbnz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbnz_d/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 64-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbnz_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbnz_h/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 16-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbnz_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbnz_v/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if the whole vector `a` is non-zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbnz_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbnz_w/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if all 32-bit elements in `a` are non-zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbz_b/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 8-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbz_d/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 64-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbz_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbz_h/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 16-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbz_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbz_v/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if the whole vector `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xbz_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xbz_w/index.md) — Expected to be used in branches: branch if any 32-bit element in `a` equals to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclr_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclr_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 64, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclr_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 16, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclr_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, clear the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 32, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclri_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclri_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclri_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclri_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclri_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclri_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitclri_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitclri_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, clear the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrev_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrev_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrev_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrev_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 64, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrev_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrev_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 16, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrev_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrev_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 32, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrevi_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrevi_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrevi_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrevi_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrevi_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrevi_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitrevi_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitrevi_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, toggle the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitsel_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitsel_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise selection: for each bit position, if the bit in `c` equals to one, copy the bit from `b` to `dst`, otherwise copy from `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitseli_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitseli_b/index.md) — Use each bit of `a` as a mask: where the bit is 1, select the corresponding bit of `imm`; where it is 0, select the corresponding bit of `b`. The result is written to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitset_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitset_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitset_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitset_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 64, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitset_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitset_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 16, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitset_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitset_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, set the bit indexed by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 32, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitseti_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitseti_b/index.md) — For each 8-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitseti_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitseti_d/index.md) — For each 64-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitseti_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitseti_h/index.md) — For each 16-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbitseti_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbitseti_w/index.md) — For each 32-bit element, set the bit indexed by `imm`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbsll_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbsll_v/index.md) — Shift each 128-bit lane of `a` left by `(imm % 16) * 8` bits, filling the vacated bits with zeros.
- [\_\_lasx_xvbsrl_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvbsrl_v/index.md) — Shift each 128-bit lane of `a` right by `(imm % 16) * 8` bits, filling the vacated bits with zeros.

## LASX_C (14)

- [\_\_lasx_cast_128](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_cast_128/index.md) — Return a 256-bit integer vector whose low 128-bit half contains `a`. The high 128-bit half is unspecified.
- [\_\_lasx_cast_128_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_cast_128_d/index.md) — Return a 256-bit double precision vector whose low 128-bit half contains `a`. The high 128-bit half is unspecified.
- [\_\_lasx_cast_128_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_cast_128_s/index.md) — Return a 256-bit single precision vector whose low 128-bit half contains `a`. The high 128-bit half is unspecified.
- [\_\_lasx_concat_128](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_concat_128/index.md) — Concatenate two 128-bit integer vectors. `lo` becomes the low 128-bit half and `hi` becomes the high 128-bit half.
- [\_\_lasx_concat_128_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_concat_128_d/index.md) — Concatenate two 128-bit double precision vectors. `lo` becomes the low 128-bit half and `hi` becomes the high 128-bit half.
- [\_\_lasx_concat_128_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_concat_128_s/index.md) — Concatenate two 128-bit single precision vectors. `lo` becomes the low 128-bit half and `hi` becomes the high 128-bit half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclo_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclo_b/index.md) — Count leading ones of 8-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclo_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclo_d/index.md) — Count leading ones of 64-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclo_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclo_h/index.md) — Count leading ones of 16-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclo_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclo_w/index.md) — Count leading ones of 32-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclz_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclz_b/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 8-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclz_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclz_d/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 64-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclz_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclz_h/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 16-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvclz_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvclz_w/index.md) — Count leading zeros of 32-bit elements in `a`.

- [\_\_lasx_xvdiv_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvdiv_du/index.md) — Divide each unsigned 64-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element in `b`.

## LASX_E (20)

- [\_\_lasx_extract_128_hi](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_extract_128_hi/index.md) — Extract the high 128-bit half of `a` as an integer vector.
- [\_\_lasx_extract_128_hi_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_extract_128_hi_d/index.md) — Extract the high 128-bit half of `a` as a double precision vector.
- [\_\_lasx_extract_128_hi_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_extract_128_hi_s/index.md) — Extract the high 128-bit half of `a` as a single precision vector.
- [\_\_lasx_extract_128_lo](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_extract_128_lo/index.md) — Extract the low 128-bit half of `a` as an integer vector.
- [\_\_lasx_extract_128_lo_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_extract_128_lo_d/index.md) — Extract the low 128-bit half of `a` as a double precision vector.
- [\_\_lasx_extract_128_lo_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_extract_128_lo_s/index.md) — Extract the low 128-bit half of `a` as a single precision vector.
- [\_\_lasx_xvexth_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvexth_d_w/index.md) — Sign-extend 32-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 64-bit.

- [\_\_lasx_xvexth_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvexth_h_b/index.md) — Sign-extend 8-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 16-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvexth_hu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvexth_hu_bu/index.md) — Zero-extend 8-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 16-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvexth_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvexth_q_d/index.md) — Sign-extend 64-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvexth_qu_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvexth_qu_du/index.md) — Zero-extend 64-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvexth_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvexth_w_h/index.md) — Sign-extend 16-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 32-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvexth_wu_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvexth_wu_hu/index.md) — Zero-extend 16-bit elements in the higher half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 32-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvextl_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvextl_q_d/index.md) — Sign-extend 64-bit elements in the lower half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvextl_qu_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvextl_qu_du/index.md) — Zero-extend 64-bit elements in the lower half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to 128-bit.
- [\_\_lasx_xvextrins_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvextrins_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, copy the 8-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[3:0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[7:4]`; other elements remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvextrins_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvextrins_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, copy the 64-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[4]`; other elements remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvextrins_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvextrins_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, copy the 16-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[2:0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[6:4]`; other elements remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvextrins_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvextrins_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, copy the 32-bit element of `b` selected by `imm[1:0]` into the element of `a` selected by `imm[5:4]`; other elements remain unchanged.

## LASX_F (140)

- [\_\_lasx_xvfadd_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfadd_d/index.md) — Add corresponding double precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfadd_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfadd_s/index.md) — Add corresponding single precision floating point elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfclass_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfclass_d/index.md) — Classify each double precision floating point element in `a` according to IEEE 754 `class(x)`, writing the 10-bit classification result into the corresponding element of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfclass_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfclass_s/index.md) — Classify each single precision floating point element in `a` according to IEEE 754 `class(x)`, writing the 10-bit classification result into the corresponding element of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_caf_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_caf_d/index.md) — Compare corresponding double precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if AF(Always False), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_caf_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_caf_s/index.md) — Compare corresponding single precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if AF(Always False), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_ceq_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_ceq_d/index.md) — Compare corresponding double precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if EQ(Equal), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_ceq_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_ceq_s/index.md) — Compare corresponding single precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if EQ(Equal), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_cle_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_cle_d/index.md) — Compare corresponding double precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if LE(Less than or Equal), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_cle_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_cle_s/index.md) — Compare corresponding single precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if LE(Less than or Equal), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_clt_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_clt_d/index.md) — Compare corresponding double precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if LT(Less than), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_clt_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_clt_s/index.md) — Compare corresponding single precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if LT(Less than), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_cne_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_cne_d/index.md) — Compare corresponding double precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if NE(Not Equal), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfcmp_cne_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcmp_cne_s/index.md) — Compare corresponding single precision elements in `a` and `b`, save the comparison result (all ones if NE(Not Equal), all zeros otherwise) into `dst`. No exception is raised for QNaN.

- [\_\_lasx_xvfcvtl_s_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfcvtl_s_h/index.md) — Convert half precision floating point elements in lower half of every 128-bit vector of `a` to single precision.

- [\_\_lasx_xvfrecip_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfrecip_s/index.md) — Compute reciprocal of single precision floating point elements in `a`.

- [\_\_lasx_xvfrsqrte_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfrsqrte_d/index.md) — Compute estimated reciprocal of square root of double precision floating point elements in `a`.

- [\_\_lasx_xvfrstp_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfrstp_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 8-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by the first element of the corresponding lane of `c` modulo 16, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfrstp_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfrstp_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 16-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by the first element of the corresponding lane of `c` modulo 8, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfrstpi_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfrstpi_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 8-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by `imm` modulo 16, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfrstpi_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfrstpi_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, find the first negative 16-bit element in `b`. Write its index into the lane of `a` selected by `imm` modulo 8, or write the maximum valid index plus one if no such element exists. Other elements of `a` remain unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfscaleb_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfscaleb_d/index.md) — Compute IEEE 754 scaleB (multiply by 2^`b`) of double precision floating point elements in `a` using integer exponents in `b`. Currently undocumented.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfscaleb_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfscaleb_s/index.md) — Compute IEEE 754 scaleB (multiply by 2^`b`) of single precision floating point elements in `a` using integer exponents in `b`. Currently undocumented.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfsqrt_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfsqrt_d/index.md) — Compute square root of double precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfsqrt_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfsqrt_s/index.md) — Compute square root of single precision floating point elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfsub_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfsub_d/index.md) — Subtract corresponding double precision floating point elements in `b` from elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvfsub_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvfsub_s/index.md) — Subtract corresponding single precision floating point elements in `b` from elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftint_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftint_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftint_lu_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftint_lu_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to unsigned 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftint_w_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftint_w_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as signed 32-bit integers, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftint_w_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftint_w_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 32-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftint_wu_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftint_wu_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to unsigned 32-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftinth_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftinth_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftintl_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftintl_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, using current rounding mode specified in `fscr`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftintrm_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftintrm_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftintrm_w_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftintrm_w_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, convert double-precision floating point elements in the corresponding lane of `a` into the upper half and elements in `b` into the lower half, as signed 32-bit integers, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftintrm_w_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftintrm_w_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 32-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftintrmh_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftintrmh_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in higher part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftintrml_l_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftintrml_l_s/index.md) — Convert single-precision floating point elements in lower part of every 128-bit vector of `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards negative infinity.
- [\_\_lasx_xvftintrne_l_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvftintrne_l_d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point elements in `a` to signed 64-bit integer, rounding towards nearest even.

## LASX_H (17)

- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_d_w/index.md) — Add odd-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `b`, sign-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_du_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_du_wu/index.md) — Add odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `b`, zero-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_h_b/index.md) — Add odd-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `b`, sign-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_hu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_hu_bu/index.md) — Add odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `b`, zero-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_q_d/index.md) — Add odd-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `b`, sign-extending both to 128-bit, to get the 128-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_qu_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_qu_du/index.md) — Add odd-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `b`, zero-extending both to 128-bit, to get the 128-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_w_h/index.md) — Add odd-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `b`, sign-extending both to 32-bit, to get the 32-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhaddw_wu_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhaddw_wu_hu/index.md) — Add odd-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a` to even-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `b`, zero-extending both to 32-bit, to get the 32-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhseli_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhseli_d/index.md) — Select each double word of `dst` from an adjacent pair in `a` according to `imm`: bits 0 and 1 choose the sources for D[0] and D[1] from {a.D[0], a.D[1]}, and bits 2 and 3 choose the sources for D[2] and D[3] from {a.D[2], a.D[3]}.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_d_w/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned signed 32-bit elements in `a`, sign-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_du_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_du_wu/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned unsigned 32-bit elements in `a`, zero-extending both to 64-bit, to get the 64-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_h_b/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned signed 8-bit elements in `a`, sign-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_hu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_hu_bu/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned unsigned 8-bit elements in `a`, zero-extending both to 16-bit, to get the 16-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_q_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_q_d/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned signed 64-bit elements in `a`, sign-extending both to 128-bit, to get the 128-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_qu_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_qu_du/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned unsigned 64-bit elements in `a`, zero-extending both to 128-bit, to get the 128-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_w_h/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `a`, sign-extending both to 32-bit, to get the 32-bit result.
- [\_\_lasx_xvhsubw_wu_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvhsubw_wu_hu/index.md) — Subtract even-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `b` from odd-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `a`, zero-extending both to 32-bit, to get the 32-bit result.

## LASX_I (18)

- [\_\_lasx_insert_128_hi](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_insert_128_hi/index.md) — Replace the high 128-bit half of `a` with the integer vector `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_insert_128_hi_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_insert_128_hi_d/index.md) — Replace the high 128-bit half of `a` with the double precision vector `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_insert_128_hi_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_insert_128_hi_s/index.md) — Replace the high 128-bit half of `a` with the single precision vector `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_insert_128_lo](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_insert_128_lo/index.md) — Replace the low 128-bit half of `a` with the integer vector `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_insert_128_lo_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_insert_128_lo_d/index.md) — Replace the low 128-bit half of `a` with the double precision vector `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_insert_128_lo_s](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_insert_128_lo_s/index.md) — Replace the low 128-bit half of `a` with the single precision vector `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvh_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvh_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 8-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvh_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvh_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 64-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvh_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvh_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 16-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvh_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvh_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 32-bit elements in the upper half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvl_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvl_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 8-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvl_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvl_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 64-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvl_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvl_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 16-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvilvl_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvilvl_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, interleave the 32-bit elements in the lower half of that lane from `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvinsgr2vr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvinsgr2vr_d/index.md) — Insert the low 64 bits of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvinsgr2vr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvinsgr2vr_w/index.md) — Insert the low 32 bits of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvinsve0_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvinsve0_d/index.md) — Insert the first 64-bit element of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.
- [\_\_lasx_xvinsve0_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvinsve0_w/index.md) — Insert the first 32-bit element of `b` into lane `imm` of `a`; the other lanes are unchanged.

## LASX_L (7)

- [\_\_lasx_xvld](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvld/index.md) — Read whole vector from memory address `addr + offset`, save the data into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. Note that you can use this intrinsic to load floating point vectors, even though the return type represents integer vectors.
- [\_\_lasx_xvldi](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvldi/index.md) — Initialize `dst` using predefined patterns:
- [\_\_lasx_xvldrepl_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvldrepl_b/index.md) — Read 8-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lasx_xvldrepl_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvldrepl_d/index.md) — Read 64-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lasx_xvldrepl_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvldrepl_h/index.md) — Read 16-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lasx_xvldrepl_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvldrepl_w/index.md) — Read 32-bit data from memory address `addr + offset`, replicate the data to all vector lanes and save into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. The `offset` operand is a byte offset, matching the assembly immediate; the encoded instruction stores it as a scaled immediate for wider elements.
- [\_\_lasx_xvldx](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvldx/index.md) — Read whole vector from memory address `addr + offset`, save the data into `dst`. Address misalignment does not raise an exception. Note that you can use this intrinsic to load floating point vectors, even though the return type represents integer vectors.

## LASX_M (115)

- [\_\_lasx_xvmadd_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvmadd_b/index.md) — Multiply 8-bit elements in `b` and `c`, add the low 8 bits of the product to elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvmadd_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvmadd_d/index.md) — Multiply 64-bit elements in `b` and `c`, add the low 64 bits of the product to elements in `a`, save the result in `dst`.

## LASX_N (6)

- [\_\_lasx_xvneg_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvneg_b/index.md) — Negate 8-bit elements in `a` and save the result in `dst`.

- [\_\_lasx_xvneg_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvneg_h/index.md) — Negate 16-bit elements in `a` and save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvneg_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvneg_w/index.md) — Negate 32-bit elements in `a` and save the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvnor_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvnor_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise NOR (~(a | b)) of elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvnori_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvnori_b/index.md) — Compute bitwise NOR (~(a | imm)) of elements in `a` and `imm`.

- [\_\_lasx_xvor_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvor_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise OR of elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvori_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvori_b/index.md) — Compute bitwise OR of elements in `a` and `imm`.
