## LASX_P (32)

- [\_\_lasx_xvpackev_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackev_b/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 8-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpackev_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackev_d/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpackev_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackev_h/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpackev_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackev_w/index.md) — Interleave even-positioned 32-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpackod_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackod_b/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 8-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpackod_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackod_d/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpackod_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackod_h/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpackod_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpackod_w/index.md) — Interleave odd-positioned 32-bit elements of `a` and `b`: the element from `b` is placed at the even output position and the element from `a` at the following odd position.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpcnt_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpcnt_b/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 8-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpcnt_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpcnt_d/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 64-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpcnt_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpcnt_h/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 16-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpcnt_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpcnt_w/index.md) — Count the number of ones (population, popcount) in 32-bit elements in `a`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvperm_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvperm_w/index.md) — For each word, use the low 3 bits of the corresponding word in `b` as an index into the eight words of `a`, and store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpermi_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpermi_d/index.md) — Select four double words from `a` using `imm[1:0]`, `imm[3:2]`, `imm[5:4]`, and `imm[7:6]`, and store them in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpermi_q](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpermi_q/index.md) — Select the two quad words of `dst` from the four quad words of `a` and `b` using `imm[1:0]` and `imm[5:4]`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpermi_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpermi_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, use `imm[1:0]` and `imm[3:2]` to select words 0 and 1 from `b`, and `imm[5:4]` and `imm[7:6]` to select words 2 and 3 from `a`; store the result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickev_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickev_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place even-positioned 8-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and even-positioned 8-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickev_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickev_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place even-positioned 64-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and even-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickev_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickev_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place even-positioned 16-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and even-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickev_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickev_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place even-positioned 32-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and even-positioned 32-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickod_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickod_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place odd-positioned 8-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and odd-positioned 8-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickod_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickod_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place odd-positioned 64-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and odd-positioned 64-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickod_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickod_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place odd-positioned 16-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and odd-positioned 16-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickod_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickod_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, place odd-positioned 32-bit elements of `b` into its lower half and odd-positioned 32-bit elements of `a` into its upper half.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve2gr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve2gr_d/index.md) — Pick the 64-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, sign-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve2gr_du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve2gr_du/index.md) — Pick the 64-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, zero-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve2gr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve2gr_w/index.md) — Pick the 32-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, sign-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve2gr_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve2gr_wu/index.md) — Pick the 32-bit element at lane `idx` from `a`, zero-extend it to the GPR width, and store it in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve_d/index.md) — Copy one 64-bit lane from `a` specified by `imm` to the first lane of `dst`, and set the other lanes to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve_d_f](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve_d_f/index.md) — Copy one 64-bit lane from `a` specified by `imm` to the first lane of `dst`, and set the other lanes to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve_w/index.md) — Copy one 32-bit lane from `a` specified by `imm` to the first lane of `dst`, and set the other lanes to zero.
- [\_\_lasx_xvpickve_w_f](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvpickve_w_f/index.md) — Copy one 32-bit lane from `a` specified by `imm` to the first lane of `dst`, and set the other lanes to zero.

## LASX_R (29)

- [\_\_lasx_xvrepl128vei_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepl128vei_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrepl128vei_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepl128vei_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrepl128vei_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepl128vei_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrepl128vei_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepl128vei_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplgr2vr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplgr2vr_b/index.md) — Broadcast the low 8 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplgr2vr_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplgr2vr_d/index.md) — Broadcast the low 64 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplgr2vr_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplgr2vr_h/index.md) — Broadcast the low 16 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplgr2vr_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplgr2vr_w/index.md) — Broadcast the low 32 bits of `val` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrepli_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepli_b/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrepli_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepli_d/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrepli_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepli_h/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrepli_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrepli_w/index.md) — Repeat `imm` to fill whole vector.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve0_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve0_b/index.md) — Repeat the first 8-bit lane from `a` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve0_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve0_d/index.md) — Repeat the first 64-bit lane from `a` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve0_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve0_h/index.md) — Repeat the first 16-bit lane from `a` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve0_q](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve0_q/index.md) — Repeat the first 128-bit lane from `a` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve0_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve0_w/index.md) — Repeat the first 32-bit lane from `a` to all lanes of `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` (index modulo 16) to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` (index modulo 2) to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` (index modulo 8) to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvreplve_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvreplve_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, repeat that lane's element `idx` (index modulo 4) to fill the same lane.
- [\_\_lasx_xvrotr_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvrotr_b/index.md) — Rotate right each unsigned 8-bit element in `a` by the corresponding element of `b` modulo 8, store the result in `dst`.

- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf4i_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf4i_b/index.md) — For each group of four consecutive 8-bit elements in `a`, permute the group according to the four 2-bit fields of `imm`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf4i_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf4i_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, select two 64-bit elements from the four elements formed by the corresponding lanes of `a` and `b` using `imm[1:0]` and `imm[3:2]`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf4i_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf4i_h/index.md) — For each group of four consecutive 16-bit elements in `a`, permute the group according to the four 2-bit fields of `imm`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf4i_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf4i_w/index.md) — For each group of four consecutive 32-bit elements in `a`, permute the group according to the four 2-bit fields of `imm`, save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf_b/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle bytes from `a` and `b` using the low 5 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `c`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf_d/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle 64-bit elements from `b` and `c` using the low 2 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `a`, and save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf_h/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle 16-bit elements from `b` and `c` using the low 4 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `a`, and save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvshuf_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvshuf_w/index.md) — For each 128-bit lane, shuffle 32-bit elements from `b` and `c` using the low 3 bits of the indices from the corresponding lane of `a`, and save the result to `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvsigncov_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvsigncov_b/index.md) — If the 8-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 8-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.
- [\_\_lasx_xvsigncov_d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvsigncov_d/index.md) — If the 64-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 64-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.
- [\_\_lasx_xvsigncov_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvsigncov_h/index.md) — If the 16-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 16-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.
- [\_\_lasx_xvsigncov_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvsigncov_w/index.md) — If the 32-bit element in `a` equals to zero, set the result to zero. If the signed 32-bit element in `a` is positive, copy element in `b` to result. Otherwise, copy negated element in `b` to result. If `a` and `b` are the same vectors, it is equivalent to computing absolute value.

- [\_\_lasx_xvsubwod_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvsubwod_w_h/index.md) — Subtract the odd-positioned signed 16-bit elements in `b` from the corresponding elements in `a`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvsubwod_w_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvsubwod_w_hu/index.md) — Subtract the odd-positioned unsigned 16-bit elements in `b` from the corresponding elements in `a`, extending both to 32-bit, and save the 32-bit result in `dst`.

## LASX_V (12)

- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_d_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_d_b/index.md) — Sign-extend the lowest 4 8-bit elements of `a` to 64-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_d_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_d_h/index.md) — Sign-extend the lowest 4 16-bit elements of `a` to 64-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_d_w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_d_w/index.md) — Sign-extend the lowest 4 32-bit elements of `a` to 64-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_du_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_du_bu/index.md) — Zero-extend the lowest 4 8-bit elements of `a` to 64-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_du_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_du_hu/index.md) — Zero-extend the lowest 4 16-bit elements of `a` to 64-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_du_wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_du_wu/index.md) — Zero-extend the lowest 4 32-bit elements of `a` to 64-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_h_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_h_b/index.md) — Sign-extend the lowest 16 8-bit elements of `a` to 16-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_hu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_hu_bu/index.md) — Zero-extend the lowest 16 8-bit elements of `a` to 16-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_w_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_w_b/index.md) — Sign-extend the lowest 8 8-bit elements of `a` to 32-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_w_h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_w_h/index.md) — Sign-extend the lowest 8 16-bit elements of `a` to 32-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_wu_bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_wu_bu/index.md) — Zero-extend the lowest 8 8-bit elements of `a` to 32-bit elements.
- [\_\_lasx_vext2xv_wu_hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_vext2xv_wu_hu/index.md) — Zero-extend the lowest 8 16-bit elements of `a` to 32-bit elements.

## LASX_X (2)

- [\_\_lasx_xvxor_v](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvxor_v/index.md) — Compute bitwise XOR of elements in `a` and `b`.
- [\_\_lasx_xvxori_b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/__lasx_xvxori_b/index.md) — Compute bitwise XOR of elements in `a` and `imm`.

## A (28)

- [adc.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/adc.b/index.md) — Add 8-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with carry (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [adc.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/adc.d/index.md) — Add 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with carry (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [adc.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/adc.h/index.md) — Add 16-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with carry (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [adc.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/adc.w/index.md) — Add 32-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with carry (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [addu12i.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/addu12i.d/index.md) — Add sign-extended 5-bit immediate left-shifted by 12 to 64-bit value in `rj` and store the result in `rd`.
- [addu12i.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/addu12i.w/index.md) — Add sign-extended 5-bit immediate left-shifted by 12 to unsigned 32-bit value in `rj`, sign-extend the 32-bit result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [armadc.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armadc.w/index.md) — ARM-style add with carry (32-bit): conditionally add values in `rj` and `rk` with carry (C in ARMFLAGS). Update ARMFLAGS (C, V, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armadd.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armadd.w/index.md) — ARM-style add (32-bit): conditionally add values in `rj` and `rk`. Update ARMFLAGS (C, V, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armand.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armand.w/index.md) — ARM-style bitwise AND (32-bit): conditionally AND values in `rj` and `rk`. Update ARMFLAGS (N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armmfflag](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armmfflag/index.md) — Move from ARMFLAGS: read selected ARM flags (N, Z, C, V) from ARMFLAGS as determined by `mask`, pack them into `rd`.
- [armmov.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armmov.d/index.md) — ARM-style move (with flag update): conditionally test the 64-bit value in `rj` and update ARMFLAGS (N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armmov.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armmov.w/index.md) — ARM-style move (with flag update): conditionally test the 32-bit value in `rj` and update ARMFLAGS (N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armmove](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armmove/index.md) — ARM-style conditional move: if the ARM condition holds, copy the value in `rj` to `rd`. ARMFLAGS are not updated.
- [armmtflag](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armmtflag/index.md) — Move to ARMFLAGS: write selected ARM flags (N, Z, C, V) in ARMFLAGS from the corresponding bit positions in `rd`, as determined by `mask`.
- [armnot.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armnot.w/index.md) — ARM-style bitwise NOT (32-bit): conditionally bitwise NOT the value in `rj`. Update ARMFLAGS (N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armor.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armor.w/index.md) — ARM-style bitwise OR (32-bit): conditionally OR values in `rj` and `rk`. Update ARMFLAGS (N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armrotr.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armrotr.w/index.md) — ARM-style rotate right (32-bit): conditionally rotate value in `rj` right by amount in `rk` (masked to 8 bits, rotate modulo 32). Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armrotri.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armrotri.w/index.md) — ARM-style rotate right immediate (32-bit): conditionally rotate value in `rj` right by `imm`. Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armrrx.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armrrx.w/index.md) — ARM-style rotate right extended (32-bit): conditionally rotate the value in `rj` and C (in ARMFLAGS) together as a 33-bit ring right by 1. Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armsbc.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armsbc.w/index.md) — ARM-style subtract with borrow (32-bit): conditionally subtract value in `rk` from `rj` with borrow (C in ARMFLAGS). Update ARMFLAGS (C, V, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armsll.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armsll.w/index.md) — ARM-style logical shift left (32-bit): conditionally shift value in `rj` left by amount in `rk` (masked to 8 bits). Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armslli.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armslli.w/index.md) — ARM-style logical shift left immediate (32-bit): conditionally shift value in `rj` left by `imm`. Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armsra.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armsra.w/index.md) — ARM-style arithmetic shift right (32-bit): conditionally shift value in `rj` right arithmetically by amount in `rk` (masked to 8 bits). Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armsrai.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armsrai.w/index.md) — ARM-style arithmetic shift right immediate (32-bit): conditionally shift value in `rj` right arithmetically by `imm`. Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armsrl.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armsrl.w/index.md) — ARM-style logical shift right (32-bit): conditionally shift value in `rj` right logically by amount in `rk` (masked to 8 bits). Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armsrli.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armsrli.w/index.md) — ARM-style logical shift right immediate (32-bit): conditionally shift value in `rj` right logically by `imm`. Update ARMFLAGS (C, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armsub.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armsub.w/index.md) — ARM-style subtract (32-bit): conditionally subtract value in `rk` from `rj`. Update ARMFLAGS (C, V, N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [armxor.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/armxor.w/index.md) — ARM-style bitwise XOR (32-bit): conditionally XOR values in `rj` and `rk`. Update ARMFLAGS (N, Z). Only ARMFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.

## F (3)

- [fcvt.d.ld](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/fcvt.d.ld/index.md) — Convert x87 80-bit extended precision value (upper 16 bits in `fj`, lower 64 bits in `fk`) to double-precision floating point, store in `fd`.
- [fcvt.ld.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/fcvt.ld.d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point value in `fj` to the lower 64 bits (integer bit and fraction) of x87 80-bit extended precision format, store in `fd`.
- [fcvt.ud.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/fcvt.ud.d/index.md) — Convert double-precision floating point value in `fj` to the upper 16 bits (sign and exponent) of x87 80-bit extended precision format, store in `fd`.

## J (2)

- [jiscr0](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/jiscr0/index.md) — Jump via scratch register 0: add `imm` to the value in SCR[0] and jump to the resulting address.
- [jiscr1](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/jiscr1/index.md) — Jump via scratch register 1: save the current PC to SCR[0], then add `imm` to the value in SCR[1] and jump to the resulting address.

## L (4)

- [ldl.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/ldl.d/index.md) — MIPS-style load doubleword left: load left part of an unaligned doubleword/word from memory at address `rj + imm`, merge with the existing value in `rd`, store the result in `rd`.
- [ldl.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/ldl.w/index.md) — MIPS-style load doubleword left: load left part of an unaligned doubleword/word from memory at address `rj + imm`, merge with the existing value in `rd`, store the result in `rd`.
- [ldr.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/ldr.d/index.md) — MIPS-style load doubleword right: load right part of an unaligned doubleword/word from memory at address `rj + imm`, merge with the existing value in `rd`, store the result in `rd`.
- [ldr.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/ldr.w/index.md) — MIPS-style load doubleword right: load right part of an unaligned doubleword/word from memory at address `rj + imm`, merge with the existing value in `rd`, store the result in `rd`.

## M (2)

- [movgr2scr](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/movgr2scr/index.md) — Move GPR to scratch register: copy value in `rj` to the scratch register selected by `scrd` (0-3). Scratch registers LBT0-LBT3 are accessible via $scr0-$scr3.
- [movscr2gr](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/movscr2gr/index.md) — Move scratch register to GPR: copy value in the scratch register selected by `scrj` (0-3) to `rd`.

## R (16)

- [rcr.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcr.b/index.md) — Rotate 8-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 9-bit ring right by the amount specified in `rk`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rcr.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcr.d/index.md) — Rotate 64-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 65-bit ring right by the amount specified in `rk`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rcr.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcr.h/index.md) — Rotate 16-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 17-bit ring right by the amount specified in `rk`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rcr.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcr.w/index.md) — Rotate 32-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 33-bit ring right by the amount specified in `rk`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rcri.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcri.b/index.md) — Rotate 8-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 9-bit ring right by immediate `imm`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rcri.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcri.d/index.md) — Rotate 64-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 65-bit ring right by immediate `imm`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rcri.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcri.h/index.md) — Rotate 16-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 17-bit ring right by immediate `imm`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rcri.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rcri.w/index.md) — Rotate 32-bit value in `rj` and CF (in EFLAGS) together as a 33-bit ring right by immediate `imm`. The result is written to `rd`.
- [rotr.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotr.b/index.md) — Rotate 8-bit value in `rj` right by the amount specified in `rk`. The shift amount is masked modulo 8.
- [rotr.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotr.d/index.md) — Rotate 64-bit value in `rj` right by the amount specified in `rk`. The shift amount is masked modulo 64.
- [rotr.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotr.h/index.md) — Rotate 16-bit value in `rj` right by the amount specified in `rk`. The shift amount is masked modulo 16.
- [rotr.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotr.w/index.md) — Rotate 32-bit value in `rj` right by the amount specified in `rk`. The shift amount is masked modulo 32.
- [rotri.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotri.b/index.md) — Rotate 8-bit value in `rj` right by immediate `imm`. The shift amount is masked modulo 8.
- [rotri.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotri.d/index.md) — Rotate 64-bit value in `rj` right by immediate `imm`. The shift amount is masked modulo 64.
- [rotri.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotri.h/index.md) — Rotate 16-bit value in `rj` right by immediate `imm`. The shift amount is masked modulo 16.
- [rotri.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/rotri.w/index.md) — Rotate 32-bit value in `rj` right by immediate `imm`. The shift amount is masked modulo 32.

## S (12)

- [sbc.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/sbc.b/index.md) — Subtract 8-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with borrow (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [sbc.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/sbc.d/index.md) — Subtract 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with borrow (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [sbc.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/sbc.h/index.md) — Subtract 16-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with borrow (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [sbc.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/sbc.w/index.md) — Subtract 32-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with borrow (CF in EFLAGS), sign-extend the result to 64-bit and store in `rd`.
- [setarmj](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/setarmj/index.md) — ARM-style set if condition: evaluate ARM condition code `cond` against ARMFLAGS. Store 1 in `rd` if the condition holds, 0 otherwise.
- [setx86j](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/setx86j/index.md) — x86-style set if condition: evaluate x86 condition code `cond` against EFLAGS. Store 1 in `rd` if the condition holds, 0 otherwise.
- [setx86loope](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/setx86loope/index.md) — x86-style loop while equal: the value in `rj` is the counter after decrement. Store 1 in `rd` if the counter is non-zero and ZF (in EFLAGS) is set, 0 otherwise.
- [setx86loopne](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/setx86loopne/index.md) — x86-style loop while not equal: the value in `rj` is the counter after decrement. Store 1 in `rd` if the counter is non-zero and ZF (in EFLAGS) is clear, 0 otherwise.
- [stl.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/stl.d/index.md) — MIPS-style store doubleword left: store left part of value in `rd` to unaligned memory address `rj + imm`.
- [stl.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/stl.w/index.md) — MIPS-style store doubleword left: store left part of value in `rd` to unaligned memory address `rj + imm`.
- [str.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/str.d/index.md) — MIPS-style store doubleword right: store right part of value in `rd` to unaligned memory address `rj + imm`.
- [str.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/str.w/index.md) — MIPS-style store doubleword right: store right part of value in `rd` to unaligned memory address `rj + imm`.

## X (113)

- [x86adc.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86adc.b/index.md) — x86-style add with carry: add 8-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with CF (in EFLAGS). Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86adc.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86adc.d/index.md) — x86-style add with carry: add 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with CF (in EFLAGS). Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86adc.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86adc.h/index.md) — x86-style add with carry: add 16-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with CF (in EFLAGS). Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86adc.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86adc.w/index.md) — x86-style add with carry: add 32-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with CF (in EFLAGS). Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86add.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86add.b/index.md) — x86-style add: add 8-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86add.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86add.d/index.md) — x86-style add: add 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86add.du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86add.du/index.md) — x86-style add: add unsigned 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86add.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86add.h/index.md) — x86-style add: add 16-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86add.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86add.w/index.md) — x86-style add: add 32-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86add.wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86add.wu/index.md) — x86-style add: add unsigned 32-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Update EFLAGS (CF, AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF) according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86and.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86and.b/index.md) — x86-style bitwise AND: compute 8-bit AND of values in `rj` and `rk`. Clear CF, OF, AF. Update PF, ZF, SF according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86and.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86and.d/index.md) — x86-style bitwise AND: compute 64-bit AND of values in `rj` and `rk`. Clear CF, OF, AF. Update PF, ZF, SF according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86and.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86and.h/index.md) — x86-style bitwise AND: compute 16-bit AND of values in `rj` and `rk`. Clear CF, OF, AF. Update PF, ZF, SF according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86and.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86and.w/index.md) — x86-style bitwise AND: compute 32-bit AND of values in `rj` and `rk`. Clear CF, OF, AF. Update PF, ZF, SF according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86clrtm](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86clrtm/index.md) — Clear x87 FPU stack translation mode: disable TOP-based remapping of FPR registers.
- [x86dec.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86dec.b/index.md) — x86-style decrement: subtract 1 from the 8-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86dec.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86dec.d/index.md) — x86-style decrement: subtract 1 from the 64-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86dec.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86dec.h/index.md) — x86-style decrement: subtract 1 from the 16-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86dec.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86dec.w/index.md) — x86-style decrement: subtract 1 from the 32-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86dectop](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86dectop/index.md) — Decrement x87 FPU stack top pointer (FDECSTP): decrement TOP by 1, wrapping modulo 8.
- [x86inc.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86inc.b/index.md) — x86-style increment: add 1 to the 8-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86inc.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86inc.d/index.md) — x86-style increment: add 1 to the 64-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86inc.h](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86inc.h/index.md) — x86-style increment: add 1 to the 16-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86inc.w](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86inc.w/index.md) — x86-style increment: add 1 to the 32-bit value in `rj`. Update EFLAGS (AF, OF, PF, ZF, SF). Preserve CF. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86inctop](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86inctop/index.md) — Increment x87 FPU stack top pointer (FINCSTP): increment TOP by 1, wrapping modulo 8.
- [x86mfflag](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mfflag/index.md) — Move from x86 EFLAGS: read selected x86 flags (CF, PF, AF, ZF, SF, OF) from EFLAGS as determined by `mask`, pack them into `rd`.
- [x86mftop](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mftop/index.md) — Move from x87 FPU stack top pointer: read the current x87 TOP (stack top index, 3-bit) into `rd`.
- [x86mtflag](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mtflag/index.md) — Move to x86 EFLAGS: write selected x86 flags (CF, PF, AF, ZF, SF, OF) in EFLAGS from the corresponding bit positions in `rd`, as determined by `mask`.
- [x86mttop](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mttop/index.md) — Move to x87 FPU stack top pointer: set the x87 TOP (stack top index, 3-bit) to `imm`.
- [x86mul.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mul.b/index.md) — x86-style multiply: multiply signed 8-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Set CF and OF if overflow (result does not fit in 8-bits), clear other flags. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86mul.bu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mul.bu/index.md) — x86-style multiply: multiply unsigned 8-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Set CF and OF if overflow (result does not fit in 8-bits), clear other flags. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86mul.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mul.d/index.md) — x86-style multiply: multiply signed 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Set CF and OF if overflow (result does not fit in 64-bits), clear other flags. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86mul.du](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mul.du/index.md) — x86-style multiply: multiply unsigned 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. - [x86mul.hu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mul.hu/index.md) — x86-style multiply: multiply unsigned 16-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. Set CF and OF if overflow (result does not fit in 16-bits), clear other flags. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.

- [x86mul.wu](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86mul.wu/index.md) — x86-style multiply: multiply unsigned 32-bit values in `rj` and `rk`. - [x86or.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86or.b/index.md) — x86-style bitwise OR: compute 8-bit OR of values in `rj` and `rk`. Clear CF, OF, AF. Update PF, ZF, SF according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86or.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86or.d/index.md) — x86-style bitwise OR: compute 64-bit OR of values in `rj` and `rk`. Clear CF, OF, AF. Update PF, ZF, SF according to the result. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.

- [x86rcl.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86rcl.b/index.md) — x86-style rotate left through carry: rotate 8-bit value in `rj` and CF together as a 9-bit ring left by the amount specified in `rk`. Update CF, OF (for shift count of 1). Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.

- [x86sbc.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86sbc.d/index.md) — x86-style subtract with borrow: subtract 64-bit values in `rj` and `rk` with CF (in EFLAGS). - [x86settag](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86settag/index.md) — Set x87 tag word bit in `rd` according to `imm1` and `imm2`. `imm2` selects the target bit position. `imm1%8` determines the operation: 0=set bit (0→1 only), 1=clear bit (1→0 only), 2-4=check tag byte then modify. Raises a BTE (Binary Translation Exception) on invalid state transitions.
- [x86settm](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86settm/index.md) — Set x87 FPU stack translation mode: enable TOP-based remapping of FPR registers (bits 0-7 are offset by TOP when TM is set).
- [x86sll.b](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86sll.b/index.md) — x86-style shift left logical: shift 8-bit value in `rj` left by the amount specified in `rk`. If shift count is non-zero, update CF, PF, ZF, SF (and OF if count is 1). If shift count is zero, flags are unchanged. Only EFLAGS (LBT4) is updated; the GPR is not modified.
- [x86sll.d](https://jia.je/unofficial-loongarch-intrinsics-guide/intrinsic/x86sll.d/index.md) — x86-style shift left logical: shift 64-bit value in `rj` left by the amount specified in `rk`.
